# Requirements traceability matrix

Authority: [FINAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SPEC.md](../FINAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SPEC.md), contract2.2.0, plus preserved repository AGENTS.md rules. This is a planning inventory, not acceptance evidence.

Each REQ identifies one exact source paragraph, numbered/list item, table row, or full example; all its conjunctive obligations bind. Counts below are **source requirement units**, not a claim that each prose sentence is an independent atomic requirement. Descriptions retain the source wording to prevent lossy omission. Repeated invariants/tests intentionally retain distinct source units. Exact original blocks, line ranges and SHA-256 are in [plan-data.json](plan-data.json); a source change fails the audit until reconciled. Code examples retain their full original payload there, rather than embedding hundreds of JSON lines in a table.

M=mandatory baseline obligation; C=conditional acceptance/authority target, required before its consumer; O=optional experiment, disabled until approved/passed; D=deferred/rejected feature, retain interfaces/disable guards, not permission to implement it. A mandatory unit mentioning an optional arm binds its limits/disabled state without enabling that arm. Priorities P0 security/integrity, P1 required quality/operations, P2 optional/deferred. Security criticality and release gates are separate. No owner approval or runtime pass is recorded.

Dependencies list predecessor tickets outside the owning ticket set and exact consumer approvals. Ticket links are scoped contributions: foundation contracts, component implementation, or final integration as specified in each ticket. A source unit spanning several tickets is accepted only after every enabled contribution and its final consumer checks; an early ticket does not need to implement later consumers to finish. Tests link the planned V-RAG registry,not implemented test nodes: materialize positive/negative assertions for each scoped contribution and record exact node IDs at handoff. Shared suite names do not by themselves prove coverage.

Inventory: **812 units**; mandatory 739, conditional 36, optional 32, deferred 5. All have owning tickets, planned verification, prerequisites/status; none is DONE.
